Run this between `/specforge-specify` and `/specforge-plan`. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-clarify.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-constitution**: Define the small set of engineering principles this project commits to. `/specforge-plan` checks every plan against them and must document compliance or an explicit exception. These are **soft gates** (advisory) — the hard, blocking gate is spec + plan approval. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-constitution.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-implement**: Execute `tasks.md` top-to-bottom to implement the active feature. This is deliberately **user-invoked only** (not auto-triggered) so building is an explicit decision. The PreToolUse gate will refuse code edits unless the feature's `specify` **and** `plan` are approved — so if you haven't approved yet, do that first. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-implement.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-init**: Create the committed `specforge/` workspace that every other SpecForge command reads and writes. Run this once per project. It is safe to re-run: never overwrite an existing file — only create what is missing and report what already existed. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-init.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-loop**: Drive the active feature's `tasks.md` to completion by executing tasks one at a time in a loop, checking each off as you go. This is SpecForge's take on the **Ralph loop** (run the agent repeatedly until the work is done) — but made **safe**: it can only ever execute a plan a human already approved, and it stops instead of guessing when it hits a blocker. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-loop.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-operate**: Close the lifecycle. After a feature is built, turn it into something that can be **shipped and owned**: a concrete deploy plan, a runbook, observability, a rollback plan, and a handoff. This is AI-DLC's **Operations** phase. It *plans* operations — it does **not** execute deployments. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-operate.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-plan**: Translate an approved spec into a concrete technical plan. This is the **Construction** design step. Consider delegating to the `architect` subagent. The human's approval of this plan is what **unblocks code edits** (together with the spec approval). ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-plan.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-quick**: The escape hatch that keeps SpecForge from becoming ceremony for a one-line fix. It flips `fasttrack: on` so the gate allows code edits without a full spec+plan — and records that choice in the audit log. **User-invoked only.** ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-quick.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-resume**: Reconstruct context from the on-disk state (which survives context compaction) and continue the lifecycle at the first unfinished step. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-resume.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-reverse-engineer**: Most real adoption is on existing code, not greenfield. This skill analyzes the current repo and produces a baseline set of artifacts so the team can move forward under the lifecycle. Delegate the heavy analysis to the `reverse-engineer` subagent to keep the main context clean. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-reverse-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-specify**: Turn a feature request into a testable specification. This is the **Inception** phase: describe the problem, users, and requirements — *not* the implementation. Delegate the drafting to the `spec-analyst` subagent when the feature is non-trivial. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-specify.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-status**: Render a clear, human-readable snapshot from `specforge/state.md` and the feature dirs. Read-only. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-status.md) · ★1)
- **specforge-tasks**: Derive small, ordered, individually verifiable tasks from `plan.md` and `spec.md`. Each task traces to the requirement(s) it satisfies so nothing is missed. ([pooyagolchian/specforge](https://github.com/pooyagolchian/specforge/blob/HEAD/dist/agents/cursor/.cursor/commands/specforge-tasks.md) · ★1)

- **speckit.product-forge.portfolio**: Cross-cutting portfolio view over every feature managed by Product Forge. Use with: "create product spec", "/speckit.product-forge.product-spec" ([B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/product-forge/commands/product-spec.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.release-readiness**: Phase 9: Pre-ship readiness checklist. Covers feature flags, rollout strategy, rollback plan, documentation, monitoring, analytics, and deployment dependencies. Use: "release readiness", "ready to ship?", "/speckit.product-forge.release-readiness" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/release-readiness.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.research**: Phase 1: Adaptive multi-dimensional feature research. Assesses input richness and auto-adjusts interview depth — minimal input triggers full 7-question interview, rich input skips to confirmation. Mandatory: competitors, UX/UI, codebase. Optional: tech stack, metrics/ROI. Saves to features/&lt;name&gt;/research/. - **speckit.product-forge.retrospective**: Post-launch retrospective comparing predicted metrics from research/metrics-roi.md against real data pulled from connected MCPs (PostHog / Amplitude product analytics, Sentry error tracking, NewRelic APM) or manual input. Closes the loop on the full product lifecycle. Run 2+ weeks after shipping. Use: "retrospective", "/speckit.product-forge.retrospective {feature-slug}" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/retrospective.md) · ★23)
- **speckit.product-forge.retrospective**: Post-launch retrospective comparing predicted metrics from research/metrics-roi.md against real data from NewRelic, Analytics MCP, or manual input. Closes the loop on the full product lifecycle. Run 2+ weeks after shipping. Use: "retrospective", "/speckit.product-forge.retrospective {feature-slug}" ([B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/product-forge/commands/retrospective.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.revalidate**: Phase 3: Iterative revalidation and correction cycle. Shows product spec summary, collects user feedback in chat, applies corrections via a dedicated agent, and loops until explicit user approval. All revision history recorded in review.md. Use with: "revalidate spec", "review product spec", "/speckit.product-forge.revalidate" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/revalidate.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.security-check**: Feature-specific OWASP security audit. Reads plan.md to detect auth, payments, user input, and file handling — then checks only the relevant attack vectors. Generates a prioritized fix list with code-level evidence. Run after implement, before ship. Use: "security check", "/speckit.product-forge.security-check" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/security-check.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.spec-merge**: Cross-cutting (living spec): merge a completed feature's delta specs (ADDED/MODIFIED/REMOVED requirements) into the canonical specs/ source of truth, then archive the change folder with audit history. Makes the spec spec-anchored — a living source of truth across the feature's lifetime, not a per-change artifact. Use with: "merge spec", "archive change", "/speckit.product-forge.spec-merge" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/spec-merge.md) · ★23)
- **speckit.product-forge.status**: Show the current Product Forge lifecycle status for a feature. Reads .forge-status.yml, displays all phases with completion state, lists available artifacts, and recommends the next action. Works for any feature in the features/ directory. Use with: "forge status", "show status", "/speckit.product-forge.status" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/status.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.sync-verify**: Cross-cutting artifact consistency checker. Verifies alignment across ALL lifecycle layers — research ↔ product-spec ↔ spec.md ↔ plan.md ↔ tasks.md ↔ code. Detects forward drift (earlier artifacts not reflected in later) and backward drift (later decisions that should update earlier artifacts). Runnable between ANY phases. Human-in-the-loop for every CRITICAL/WARNING resolution. Use: "sync-verify", "check consistency", "/speckit.product-forge.sync-verify" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/sync-verify.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.tasks**: Phase 5B: Generate task breakdown from plan.md with cross-validation against product-spec. Ensures every Must Have story and functional requirement has at least one task. Standalone — run after plan, before implement (or after any custom step). Use: "generate tasks", "task breakdown", "/speckit.product-forge.tasks" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/tasks.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.test-plan**: Phase 8A: Creates a comprehensive test plan from feature artifacts. Asks about test types (smoke, E2E Playwright, API/integration, regression), environment setup, credentials, auto-detects project test framework, and generates test cases mapped to user stories. Saves to features/&lt;name&gt;/testing/. Use with: "create test plan", "/speckit.product-forge.test-plan" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/test-plan.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.test-run**: Phase 8B: Executes test cases via playwright-cli (interactive browser agent), tracks bugs in bugs/&lt;BUG-NNN&gt;.md, auto-fixes P0/P1 bugs and retests, performs gap analysis when bugs require spec changes. Loop continues until all P0/P1 bugs closed and exit criteria met. Use with: "run tests", "execute tests", "/speckit.product-forge.test-run" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/test-run.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.tracking-plan**: Generates an analytics tracking plan from user journeys and product-spec. - **speckit.product-forge.verify-full**: Phase 7: Full traceability verification across the entire Product Forge chain. Consumes the live traceability.yml matrix and checks: code ↔ tasks ↔ plan ↔ spec.md ↔ product-spec ↔ research, plus journey↔E2E coverage, UI↔design-system, FE↔BE contract drift, and doc↔code reconciliation.
